Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the complex structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 158. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 329 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to complex theory, we decompose the problem into 158 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 329. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
